I'd like to make it clear that the Kurds are our brothers -- in no way, not in the outside -- that we accept 83 million in one, especially underlined. On the other hand, these studies of military service... as you know, a new law was made, and the law issued in 1927 was riddled with holes. After a thorough study, a law was passed, by visiting all of our parties, for one integrity, for one justice, for one equality. This law came out in June, and by November, it was the application of our expensive young people; and they did not, and now they say, "Aff. " So these inputs and exits on the system... there's a lot of demands, so we're following the situation, so we're not trying to get into a similar situation in the Military Law, which has been unmanageable since 1920. On the other hand, about the Machine and Chemical Industry Agency, we have continued our work as the Ministry within ourselves as the Ministry, the Machine and Chemistry, but in any case, a work has now been launched between the ministries, which would be a precedent for other institutions and institutions if it was put into practice only as Machines and Chemicals. Our work is ready for this work, and it will result in the completion of the work of other ministries within the framework of equality, justice, equal work, equal rights in the coming period. Other than that, sir, there was the subject of these deaths, and it was in the middle. There's nothing in our Turkish Armed Forces, sir, in the United States Department of Defense, there's no such thing as a dead person, no matter who, no matter who, no matter what, sir, or a conflict, or any kind of cover-up during an operation, there's no such thing as not showing it, sir. 100% administrative investigations are conducted, followed by forensic investigations. Other than that, there's no procedure, there's no such thing as a mind-blowing thing, the whole world can't make it happen. In addition, our other activities continue in a similar way. I pay my respects.
